## المجموعة 198: حارس المرمى 1: البطاقة المرجعية

**الدفعة 1** (السبرنت 2 (الصور) و3 (المحاكاة))  
**المجلد:** `art/raw/keeper/`  
**المحادثة:** جديدة. حارس بكل الحركات: من الجانب والأمام والخلف. ابدأ بها: كل مجموعات هذه الشخصية ترفقها.

### 198.1 `ref.png`

البطاقة المرجعية: أمام، ثلاثة أرباع، جانب، خلف  
**بلا مرفق** (أول صورة)

```
A wide 3:2 landscape character reference card: exactly four full body views of the same one person side by side in one row, no more and no fewer, at exactly the same scale, standing on the same ground line, relaxed with arms slightly away from the body, nothing cut off. The character is a fictional male goalkeeper in a plain pure white long-sleeve shirt, white shorts, white socks, black boots and big black gloves, no logos or numbers (the game paints the keeper colours on the white), short black hair, tanned skin, clean shaven, tall goalkeeper build. From left to right: front view facing the camera, three-quarter view facing right, side view facing right, back view. There is no ball anywhere in the image, because the game draws the ball itself: where a pose mentions the ball, the body moves exactly as if the ball were there, but the ball is not drawn. Photorealistic 3D render in the style of a modern console football game, lit by night stadium floodlights from high above and slightly behind: cool bluish white light with a bright rim light on the head, shoulders and arms, and slightly darker lower legs. Correct, natural anatomy in every pose: knees and elbows bend the natural way, both legs and both arms complete, feet pointing where the body moves, five fingers on each hand. The background is one flat, solid chroma-key green (#00FF00) with nothing else on it, no floor and no shadows, so each figure is easy to cut out. The image has no text, no numbers and no logos.
```

### 198.2 `faces.png`

الوجه: تركيز، فرحة، حسرة، غضب  
**ارفق:** `art/raw/keeper/ref.png`

```
Use the attached reference card for this exact same person. A wide 3:2 landscape image with exactly four close-up portraits of him in a 2 by 2 grid, head and shoulders, all of the same person, three-quarter view facing right, each with a blurred night stadium and floodlights behind, separated by thin black lines. The character is a fictional male goalkeeper in a plain pure white long-sleeve shirt, white shorts, white socks, black boots and big black gloves, no logos or numbers (the game paints the keeper colours on the white), short black hair, tanned skin, clean shaven, tall goalkeeper build. Top left: focused and calm. Top right: screaming with joy. Bottom left: devastated, hands on head. Bottom right: angry, protesting. Photorealistic 3D render like a modern console football game, lit by cool stadium floodlights with a rim light on the hair. The image has no text, no numbers and no logos.
```

## المجموعة 199: حارس المرمى 1: الاستعداد

**المحادثة:** جديدة. كل لوحة 3 إطارات متتالية (2 للحركات العريضة). ارفق البطاقة المرجعية، ومن الإطار الثاني فصاعداً ارفق معها اللوحة السابقة.

### 199.1 `ready-01.png`

keeper ready bounce: الإطارات 1 إلى 3 من 4  
**ارفق:** `art/raw/keeper/ref.png`

```
Use the attached reference card for this exact same person: the same face, hair, body and clothes, at the same scale. A wide 3:2 landscape image with 3 animation frames side by side in one row: frames 1 to 3 of a 4-frame keeper ready bounce sequence. The image shows exactly 3 figures of this same one person, one per frame, from left to right in frame order: no more, no fewer, and no other people. The character is a fictional male goalkeeper in a plain pure white long-sleeve shirt, white shorts, white socks, black boots and big black gloves, no logos or numbers (the game paints the keeper colours on the white), short black hair, tanned skin, clean shaven, tall goalkeeper build. He is seen exactly from the side, his body and face turned to the right, so the camera sees his right side: his right leg and right arm are the near ones, closest to the camera, and his left leg and left arm are the far ones; whenever he moves, he moves to the right. These are consecutive animation frames for a 2D game sprite sheet, evenly spaced in time: each frame moves on one clear step of the motion from the one before, like a real animation cycle, and no two frames are the same. Every frame shows the whole figure from head to feet at exactly the same scale, standing on the same invisible ground line (the ground line is at the same height in every frame), with clear space between frames and nothing cut off. Each frame is a clearly different pose from the others in this image; never repeat a pose. Left frame (frame 1): In the ready stance, knees bent, arms out, hands open. Middle frame (frame 2): Bouncing lightly on his toes, slightly up. Right frame (frame 3): Settling down again. There is no ball anywhere in the image, because the game draws the ball itself: where a pose mentions the ball, the body moves exactly as if the ball were there, but the ball is not drawn. Photorealistic 3D render in the style of a modern console football game, lit by night stadium floodlights from high above and slightly behind: cool bluish white light with a bright rim light on the head, shoulders and arms, and slightly darker lower legs. Correct, natural anatomy in every pose: knees and elbows bend the natural way, both legs and both arms complete, feet pointing where the body moves, five fingers on each hand. The background is one flat, solid chroma-key green (#00FF00) with nothing else on it, no floor and no shadows, so each figure is easy to cut out. The image has no text, no numbers and no logos.
```
